families / research

research

academic-research-workflow

A suite of tools for conducting, refining, and communicating scholarly research from literature discovery to manuscript polishing.

These skills differ by their specific stage in the research lifecycle, ranging from initial literature search and screening to final writing refinement and professional communication.

  • Find recent papers on CRISPR ethics and draft a polite email to a potential co-author.
  • Polish the tone of my discussion section and prepare a summary for a conference presentation.

causal-inference-methods

A collection of skills for applying statistical and machine learning techniques to estimate causal effects from observational data.

These skills differ by their specific implementation language (R vs Python), underlying library (DoWhy, CausalML, EconML), or focus on theoretical identification strategies versus practical experimental execution.

  • Estimate the average treatment effect of a marketing campaign using the DoWhy library in Python.
  • Identify the appropriate causal identification strategy for a dataset with unobserved confounders using the CausalML framework.

diverga-research

A suite of specialized research skills for conducting divergent analysis, medical consultation, and multi-stage academic inquiry.

The skills differ by their specific focus, ranging from general divergent analysis (diverga) to specialized medical advice (doctor) and various sub-components (a1, b2, etc.) that handle distinct stages of the research process.

  • Perform a divergent analysis on the provided dataset to identify outliers.
  • Consult the doctor skill to summarize the latest clinical guidelines for the condition.

empirical-analysis-workflow

A coordinated system for executing end-to-end empirical research, from data discovery and codebook generation to statistical analysis in Python, Stata, or R.

These skills differ by their specific role in the pipeline, ranging from high-level orchestration and coordination to language-specific execution engines and data preparation utilities.

  • Run a full empirical analysis on the provided dataset using Stata and generate a codebook.
  • Coordinate the research process to find relevant data and execute a Python-based statistical model.

statistical-analysis

A collection of skills for performing statistical modeling, hypothesis testing, and power analysis using various software tools and methodologies.

These skills differ by their specific focus on either the theoretical framework of statistical methods, the practical implementation in specific software like Stata, or the strategic planning of study design and power analysis.

  • Calculate the required sample size for a clinical trial with a specific power and effect size.
  • Run a linear regression model in Stata and interpret the significance of the coefficients.

econometric-identification-strategies

A collection of skills for determining and documenting the identification strategy in empirical economics and finance research papers.

These skills differ by the specific academic journal or field (e.g., JFE, JHR, JBF) they target, reflecting distinct methodological norms and expectations for identification in those venues.

  • Explain the identification strategy used in a recent JFE paper on corporate governance.
  • Compare the identification approaches in a JHR study versus a JBF study on labor market outcomes.

journal-submission-workflow

A set of skills for selecting, preparing, and submitting academic manuscripts to specific journals.

These skills focus on the practical process of choosing a target journal and preparing the manuscript for submission, rather than analyzing journal metrics or reading published content.

  • Find the best journal for my paper on quantum computing and generate a cover letter.
  • Check the latest submission guidelines for Nature and format my manuscript accordingly.

literature-research-workflow

A comprehensive suite of skills for executing end-to-end academic literature research, from strategic searching and filtering to synthesis, management, and reporting.

These skills differ by their specific stage in the research pipeline, ranging from initial search strategy and filtering to deep synthesis, full-text retrieval, and final report generation or database management.

  • Develop a search strategy and filter relevant papers to create a structured literature review on CRISPR off-target effects.
  • Fetch full texts for a list of identified articles and synthesize them into a weekly research digest with evidence grading.

nature-paper-processing

A suite of specialized tools for downloading, translating, summarizing, and analyzing academic papers from Nature journals.

These skills are distinguished by their specific focus on Nature journal publications and their granular processing steps (downloading, translating, visualizing, and deep analysis) compared to general research paper tools.

  • Download the latest Nature paper on CRISPR and generate a visual summary card.
  • Translate the abstract of a specific Nature article into Chinese and provide a detailed X-ray analysis of its methodology.

peer-review-response

A set of skills for drafting, structuring, and managing responses to peer review feedback and referee reports.

These skills focus specifically on the author's side of the peer review process, dealing with how to respond to feedback, rather than conducting the review itself or general research workflows.

  • Draft a point-by-point response letter to the reviewers for my submitted manuscript.
  • Guide me through the process of rebutting specific criticisms from a journal referee.

publication-workflow-management

A set of skills for managing the end-to-end process of academic publishing, including journal selection, deadline tracking, strategic planning, and specific conference or journal submission workflows.

These skills differ by their specific focus, ranging from general strategic and logistical tools like journal recommendation and deadline tracking to highly specific procedural workflows for individual conferences and journals.

  • Recommend suitable journals for my paper on machine learning ethics and track the resubmission deadlines for my rejected manuscript.
  • Guide me through the specific submission workflow for the CHI conference and perform a QA check on my Stata code for publication.

journal-replication-standards

A set of skills for creating, auditing, and managing replication packages and data policies in accordance with specific academic journal guidelines.

The skills differ by the specific journal or organization whose replication and data policies they address, ranging from general replication-driven research to specific guidelines for journals like AEJ, JBF, and JRU.

  • Prepare a replication package for submission to the Journal of Econometrics following their specific data policy.
  • Audit an existing replication package to ensure it meets the requirements for the American Economic Journal: Applied Economics.

scholarly-literature-discovery

A set of tools and guides for querying academic databases and search engines to locate, retrieve, and analyze peer-reviewed research papers and educational resources.

These skills differ by the specific academic database or search engine they interface with, such as biomedical (PubMed, Europe PMC), general scholarly (OpenAlex, Lens), or educational (ERIC) repositories.

  • Search for recent peer-reviewed articles on machine learning in healthcare using the PubMed API.
  • Find open-access papers on climate change policy in Europe using the Europe PMC API.

  • research

web-search-engines

A collection of skills for executing web queries across various search providers, including general-purpose, privacy-focused, and multi-engine aggregators.

These skills differ by their underlying search provider, supported features like advanced operators or multi-engine aggregation, and regional focus (e.g., CN vs Global).

  • Find the latest news articles about quantum computing breakthroughs using a privacy-focused search engine.
  • Search for specific technical documentation on a website using advanced search operators and site filters.
